ParlaMint-PL_2016-11-30-sejm-31-2.u487	Thank you very much. And I would like another statement from Małgorzata Zwierzan, the club... [[Free and Solidarnia.]] Excuse me, Wheel of Messengers Free and Solidarni. I'm so sorry.
ParlaMint-PL_2016-11-30-sejm-31-2.u488	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Freedom and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the government proposal for a law amending certain laws on health and safety of the service. The purpose of the contested law is to implement the Constitutional Tribunal judgment of 24 November 2015 in which it was declared incompatible with the Constitution to be a rule of law lower than that of disposal concerning specific health and safety conditions of the service. According to current solutions, these issues are dealt with in the form of implementing acts, i.e. Orders of police and border guards officers and regulations for other services. This draft law is a proposal for applicants to properly place in the legal order detailed conditions and rules relating to the safety and hygiene of the service. The regulations contained therein provide for the appointment of superiors responsible for the health and safety of the service and for the determination of how they are ensured by subordinate officers, the imposition on officers of an obligation to comply with the rules and principles of safety and hygiene, the establishment of a legal standard giving officers and officers the same right to refrain from carrying out their duties where the conditions do not comply with the safety and hygiene rules and pose a direct risk to health or life, and the identification of cases where that standard does not apply. In addition, the regulations contained in the amendment specify the extent to which the principles of health and safety of the service set out in the Labour Code of 26 June 1974 apply to the service. Non-compliance with the constitution of the existing solutions necessitates changes. I agree with the view that the current legal situation granting police officers and officers the right to safe and hygienic conditions of service by an act of law lower than the law does not sufficiently protect their interests. The proposed project restores the proper legal order in this area. The Free and Solidarnia Members' Circle considers it appropriate to repeal the contested provisions and replace them with the new regulations set out in this Act. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

Here children from kindergartens located in rural communities will receive a subsidy of 11% bigger than preschool children, I'm talking about six-year-olds in a big city. It should be made clear that six-year-old local governments will receive more than PLN 4300. The amendments proposed by applicants, in addition to allowing funding under the part of the general education subsidy for pre-school children aged 6 years and above, also adapt the provisions on grants for the funding of pre-school education, the granting and clearance of pre-school education tasks. However, until steps are taken in this direction, I consider it useful to adapt the current regulations to the requirements and expectations of local governments, parents and pupils. ParlaMint-PL_2016-11-30-sejm-31-2.u528	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The first reading of the Government's draft law amending the Law on the provision of public-funded healthcare was held at a joint meeting of the Committee on Health and Agriculture and Rural Development on 29 November 2016. This draft law aims to introduce into the Act on the provision of public-funded health care rules governing the payment of health insurance contributions to farmers, their family members and their household members. At present, these rules are laid down in the Act of 13 January 2012 on farmers' health insurance contributions for the years 2012 to 2016, which will cease to apply on 31 December 2016. After this date, without appropriate legislative changes, farmers, members of their families and their households will not be covered by the health insurance scheme and the National Health Fund will not be able to receive health insurance contributions from farmers. The proposed law must therefore enter into force on 1 January 2017. The provisions contained in the draft are based on the solutions contained in the existing Act of 13 January 2012 and need to be amended accordingly for them to come into force on 1 January 2017. These solutions have now been functioning without interference since 2012 and the collection of contributions is high. It is therefore justified to give them the nature of the target solutions. The proposed provisions set out the amount of the monthly health insurance contribution. First, for farmers holding a holding and their household and farmers' households who are active in the field of special divisions and additionally on the holding, at a level of PLN 1 per person insured for each full hectare of conversion of agricultural land on the holding accepted for the purpose of determining the social security contributions of farmers and on agricultural holdings with an area of more than PLN 1 ha of conversion of agricultural land at a level of PLN 1. Secondly, in the case of households of farmers operating exclusively in the field of special agricultural production units, at a level of 9% , the premium is based on an amount of 33,4% Average monthly wage and salary in the enterprise sector in the 4th quarter of the previous year including profit payments. In agricultural holdings with an area of more than 6 ha in the conversion of agricultural land, contributions to the health insurance of farmers holding a holding and their household and of farmers who carry out activities in the field of special divisions and in addition on the holding and of farmers who operate exclusively in the areas of special agricultural production shall be paid by the farmer. On the other hand, health insurance contributions are paid by the Agricultural Social Insurance Fund in agricultural holdings with an area of more than 6 hectares. According to the project, the amount transferred for this purpose to the National Health Fund by KRUS amounts to 155 167 thousand. PLN per month. These are the rules so far. The rules for determining the premium base for farmers active in special divisions also remain unchanged. The project has thus far adopted different rules for setting health insurance premiums for farmers operating in special divisions and for farmers operating in the agricultural holding. The introduced differentiation meets the constitutional principle of equality, as the size of the agricultural holding has an impact on the income generated by the farmer. Therefore, for both categories of farmers, the premium base shall be determined taking into account their income opportunities. The establishment of a system for calculating the amount of the contribution for all types of farmers on a uniform basis relating to the income obtained shall be the implementation of the constitutional principle of equality in accordance with the judgment of the Constitutional Court of 26 October 2010. In addition, the proposed law introduces changes aimed at improving the process of appointing members to the councils of the voivodship branches of the fund. It is proposed that the councils of the voivodship branches of the fund be appointed by the voivodships. The transfer by the Minister of Health of competence to appoint the competent voivodeship councils will contribute to faster recruitment of members or to faster changes in the membership of the voivodship board. The councils of the voivodship branches of the fund are composed of representatives of bodies operating in the voivodship and it is justified that the recruitment is carried out also by an authority from that voivodship. It is proposed that if candidates are not indicated by authorised patient organisations after twice publication of the notice, a candidate designated by the voivode shall be appointed. This change is due to the fact that, at the time of the recruitment of members of these councils, there have been situations where, despite the publication three times in the Public Information Bulletin of the Ministry of Health, no candidate has been notified of the intention to appoint a member of the Board for the organisation of patients working for the rights of the patient. The absence of notifications was then blocked by the possibility of setting up councils for the regional branches of the fund. In view of the above, the Sejm committees request the adoption by the High Sejm of a draft law annexed to the committee's report. Thank you. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2016-11-30-sejm-31-2.u529	Thank you very much, Member. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. I ask Mr Krzysztof Szulowski to speak on behalf of the Law and Justice Club. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2016-11-30-sejm-31-2.u530	Mrs. Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, I have the honour to present the position of the Poselski Club Law and Justice on the government draft law amending the Law on healthcare services financed by public funds, print No 1057. The need to amend the Act of 13 January 2012 in force until 31 December 2012. Consequently, after that date farmers and their family members and households would not be covered by the general health insurance scheme. In addition, the National Health Fund could not receive contributions for the health insurance of farmers. In the view of the applicants, there is also a need to improve the procedure for the appointment of members of the NFZ Voivodeship Branch Boards. The proposed law is generally based on the transfer of the solutions contained in the above-mentioned Act of 13 January 2012 on contributions to the health insurance of farmers for the years 2012 and 2016 to the Law of 27 August 2004 on the provision of public-funded healthcare. The project has adopted rules on the fixing of farmers' health insurance contributions, which are different for farmers operating in the field of special divisions and for other farmers. According to the judgment of the Constitutional Court, this differentiation complies with the constitutional principle of equality, since the size of the agricultural holding defined in the conversion hectares has an impact on the amount of income generated by the farmer. For both categories of farmers, the premium base is therefore established taking into account their income opportunities. The Act sets out the amount and method of payment of health insurance premiums for farmers, excluding farmers operating exclusively in the areas of special agricultural production and farmers operating in the fields of special agricultural production and in addition in the agricultural holding, the farmers' households referred to in point 1 and the farmers' households who operate in the fields of special agricultural production and in addition in the agricultural holding, and, finally, the farmers' households operating in the exclusive scope of special agricultural production units. For the first two groups, the monthly health insurance premium for each insured person is 1 PLN per full hectare of conversion of agricultural area on the holding, on holdings with an area of less than 1 ha of contribution is 1 PLN, for the third group the premium base is 33.4% the average monthly salary in the enterprise sector in the 4th quarter of the previous year, including income payments, announced by the President of the Central Statistical Office. Health insurance contributions will be paid by farmers with their own financial resources where they have an agricultural holding above 6 hectares of conversion, in turn a contribution to the insurance of farmers where they have less than 6 hectares shall be paid by the State budget under the funds covered by the CRUS. The principle of determining the premium base for farmers active in special divisions remains unchanged. Such a farmer shall pay health insurance premiums on the declared basis of the income premium established for personal income tax of not less than the amount corresponding to the minimum wage or the minimum wage in the case of activities not subject to personal income tax. In addition, the draft law introduces changes aimed at improving the process of appointing members of the Councils of the Regional Branches of the Fund. To this end, it is proposed that the councils of the voivodship branches of the fund be appointed by the voivodships. The transfer by the Minister of Health of competence to appoint the competent voivodeship councils will contribute to faster recruitment of members or to faster changes in the membership of the voivodship board. It is also proposed that if candidates are not indicated by authorised patient organisations after twice publication of the notice, a candidate designated by the voivode should be appointed. This change is due to the fact that there were problems in this respect. The absence of notifications blocked the possibility of setting up councils for the regional branches of the fund. In summary, the proposed law will ensure continuity of health insurance for farmers, members of their families and family members, and will also improve the functioning of the councils of the regional branches of the fund, therefore the Law and Justice Club will support the draft law. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

This law, as I have already mentioned, is in force until 31 December 2016 and after that date without legislative changes, which are the subject of this debate, farmers, members of their families and their households will not be covered by the general health insurance scheme and the NFZ will not be able to receive contributions from this insurance. Therefore, the contested law must enter into force on 1 January 2017 in order not to disrupt the functioning of this whole system. Turning to the detailed arrangements contained in the submission, it should be noted that, in relation to the amount of the monthly health insurance contribution, the Act leaves the current arrangements, i.e. at PLN 1 per insured person per full hectare of conversion for farmers' social security contributions. A similar rate will apply to agricultural holdings of less than 1 ha. In turn, in the case of households farmers operating exclusively in the field of special agricultural production units, the contribution will be set at 9%. , the amount of the contribution will be the basis for the assessment of the contribution 33.4% an average monthly salary. It is worth mentioning that during the public consultation which was the subject of this project, there were proposals for a slight increase in this contribution to PLN 1.1, which would result in a 10% increase in the revenue from contributions in this area. I think it's wrong that the government didn't agree with this proposal, and yet it didn't raise it by 10%. , slightly in total as regards this amount. As regards additional cases, the project also leaves the existing rules for financing health insurance contributions. Ladies and gentlemen! The Act also introduces transitional provisions and in accordance with Article. 2 The NFZ Provincial Branch Board established before the date of entry into force of this Act will operate until the date of the establishment of the NFZ Provincial Branch Board by the competent voivodeship on the basis of the provisions of the Act as amended by Article. 1. At the same time, however, the Act does not provide for financial security for new tasks for voivodships. However, what is important is that it is another council and such further stools for wages for local PiS activists, because we know how this will end: these announcements published on the last pages, etc. Therefore, we would like once again to call on Prime Minister Beata Szydło to organise these competitions, which are subject to membership of the Board, on the Supervisory Board or, as here on the Board of the NFZ branch, on the basis of substantive criteria and not on the basis of familiarity. Personnel selection based on non-essential criteria, ladies and gentlemen, spoils our country, spoils its institutions. To the end, I would like to point out that, on the one hand, we understand that for systemic reasons, the PiS government had to prepare and submit to the High House a draft law that maintained the existing rules. However, we believe that we should aim to reform the whole insurance system and to eliminate parallel social security systems and to eliminate parallel pension systems. Every citizen is equal to the law and must be treated by the State on the same basis. That is why we consider it necessary to remove the privileges for those entering the labour market, including, among others, those entering the labour market. in the area of health insurance, social security for farmers, as well as the change of the CRUS to a common social security system for all citizens. On the other hand, as far as this law is concerned, the Posel Club Modern will express its position in the vote. Thank you very much.

I would therefore like to make a statement concerning the patronage of these miners and dangerous professions. St. Barbara in the country is mostly associated with Upper Silesia and mining. In the 1960s, the Catholic diocese requested the approval of the patronage of St. Barbara, whose images and figures are still present both in Silesian houses and in churches and halls. At the beginning of the 21st century, some of these statues and paintings were moved from the hall of destroyed mines to museums. A Preamble for the Cult of St. Barbara sent the Pope in 1747 to the parish of St. St. Apostles Peter and Paul in Tarnowskie Mountains. Then the first fraternity of St. St. Barbara. It took the name of the Perfect Brotherhood of St. Barbara St. Miss Martyr of Christ. In front of the painting of St. Barbara prayed Silesians, a mining family, asking for a happy return of fathers, husbands and sons to their homes. According to the Christian tradition, St. Barbara is an example of the one who is brave, devoted, with principles that did not bend under the pressure of her father, who at the end led to the head beheading. We can draw much from her wisdom, from her light, from her care. It is said that St. Barbara is the one who takes care of those who are exposed to various kinds of tragic accidents during work. During World War II - and this must also be emphasized - she was the patron of the Polish underground. It was similar in modern times. Polish political underground, like the Polish occupation, asked her for protection. Kazimierz Józef Węgrzyn consecrated St. Barbara and the miners murdered on December 16, 1981, a very eloquent poem.
